 * CSP-safe: no runtime inline styles. Search/select/action modules remain isolated.
 * Validity is local HTML syntax only; never evidence of server authorization.
 */

 --aps-field-surface:#fff;
 --aps-field-ink:#2f4265;
 --aps-field-muted:#8796ad;

}

 color:var(--aps-field-ink);
 font-family:Inter,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI",sans-serif;

  border-color .16s var(--aps-field-ease),

}
